TL;DR: 提出ContextReID：利用同帧队友/对手与跨帧观测的空间与时间上下文，生成上下文感知的球员嵌入，显著提升体育转播中的球员ReID（Rank-1与mAP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育转播中同队球员外观高度相似，单人裁剪的ReID易混淆；而视频提供跨视角与同帧多球员的重复观测，蕴含可消歧的上下文，但现有方法大多忽略。

Method: 提出ContextReID：以视觉-语言模型为骨干，输入为同一帧的多球员裁剪集合与跨帧观测，生成对个体球员的上下文感知嵌入。训练采用结合的“上下文学习+检索”目标，既建模跨帧身份关系，又保持个体可判别性。

Result: 在三个多目标跟踪基准上，相比单裁剪嵌入方法，ContextReID在Rank-1与mAP上均有提升。

Conclusion: 空间同帧关系与时间跨帧信息对体育转播场景中的可靠球员识别至关重要；上下文显式建模能稳健提升ReID性能。

Abstract: Person re-identification is typically addressed by learning identity-discriminative embeddings from individual person crops. However, player re-identification in broadcast sports videos presents a unique challenge: players from the same team often share similar appearances, while video sequences provide repeated observations of the same players under different viewpoints and surrounding contexts. Existing crop-based approaches largely ignore this contextual information, which can be critical for resolving visually similar identities.
  Here, we propose ContextReID, a contextualized embedding framework that represents a player by jointly considering other players in the same frame and observations from different frames of the broadcast sequence. By incorporating both co-occurring players and cross-frame observations, the model learns identity features relative to the surrounding visual context rather than relying only on isolated appearance cues. We implement this framework with a vision-language model that takes sets of player crops as input and generates context-aware embeddings for individual players. A combined contextual learning and retrieval objective encourages the model to capture cross-frame identity relationships while maintaining discriminative embeddings for player matching.
  Experiments across three multi-object tracking benchmarks show that ContextReID improves player retrieval over conventional single-crop embedding approaches in both Rank-1 and mAP. These results highlight the utility of spatial and temporal context for reliable player identification in broadcast sports videos.

</details>


### [68] [SheafPrune: Sheaf-Guided Graph Propagation for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13263)
*Xiaoyang Ma,Chi Wang,Hong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SheafPrune：在多模态大模型中通过基于图的相关性传播与度校正，选择少量最重要的视觉token，以在基本不损精度下显著降低prefill与FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM通常对全部视觉token做预填充，成本高且包含大量与指令无关的token；现有基于token显著性的选择方法难以建模token间关系，导致重要性传递与排序不佳。需要一种能利用跨模态与视觉内部结构、可训练且通用的token选择机制，以在不同预算下提升速度-精度权衡。

Method: 构建稀疏文本-视觉图用于跨模态相关性转移，并在单独的视觉图上进行支持受限的分数传播，同时对节点度进行校正；受sheaf几何启发，采用模态特定映射保证一致性；在冻结的LLaVA-NeXT-8B等骨干上端到端训练仅约4.5M参数的选择器，仅使用语言建模目标；显式保留率条件化，使单个checkpoint适配不同预算；在进入冻结LLM前一次性硬性保留top-k视觉token。

Result: 在4个骨干、10个基准、7个裁剪比例的统一协议下，SheafPrune在28个骨干-预算组合中的22个取得最佳精度保留，且在中到激进裁剪下优势单调增大；在LLaVA-NeXT上仅保留10%视觉token即可实现5.15×净prefill加速与84.0%端到端FLOPs下降；消融实证支持图约束传播与度校正设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于图的相关性传播与度校正为视觉token选择提供稳健框架，SheafPrune在多种骨干与分词方案下改进精度-成本折中，并以极小额外参数在单checkpoint覆盖多预算，适合在多模态LLM推理中广泛应用。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models process the complete visual token sequence, increasing prefill cost even when many tokens are irrelevant to the instruction. Token-wise saliency captures limited inter-token dependence; graph-based selection instead depends on how relations govern importance propagation and ranking. We study support-constrained propagation with node-degree correction. Inspired by sheaf geometry, SheafPrune uses modality-specific maps and a sparse text--visual graph to transfer relevance, then propagates scores on a separate visual graph and corrects them for degree. It retains a hard top-$k$ subset once before the frozen language model. The selector has about $4.5$M parameters and is trained end-to-end through the frozen LLaVA-NeXT-8B backbone using only the language-modeling objective. Explicit retention-ratio conditioning lets one checkpoint serve every budget. Across four backbones, ten benchmarks and seven pruning ratios, with all baselines re-implemented under one protocol, SheafPrune achieves the best retention at $22$ of $28$ backbone--budget settings, with monotonically growing margins in the moderate-to-aggressive regime. On LLaVA-NeXT, retaining $10\%$ of visual tokens yields a $5.15\times$ net prefill speedup and an $84.0\%$ end-to-end FLOPs reduction. Together with component ablations, these results support graph-constrained importance propagation and degree correction as a design for visual token selection across the evaluated backbones and tokenization schemes, improving the trade-off between accuracy retention and inference cost. code:https://github.com/Bob0817912/SheafPrune_ICLR_2027

</details>


### [69] [CV-OPD: Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13264)
*Haowei Wang,Hao Lin,Qi Zhu,Jiayi Ji,Deyi Ji,Liujuan Cao,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CV-OPD以修复OPD蒸馏中“看不见也会答”的捷径问题：通过正负裁剪对比教师分布、引入对比发散损失与差异感知门，显著提升VLM的视觉依赖与细粒度推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OPD在后训练时以答案为线索提供密集监督，但缺少视觉约束，学生模型能在遮挡/噪声图像下仍答对，说明形成了不依赖视觉证据的捷径，削弱了VLM的真实视觉推理能力。

Method: 1) 对比式视觉OPD：在相同学生前缀下，对比由正样本裁剪与负样本（与答案无关）诱导的教师分布；2) 对比散度损失（CDL）：约束学生特征使其靠近正教师、远离负教师，纠正学生关注的视觉区域；3) 差异感知门（DAG）：基于token级与样本级分布差异，选择视觉相关token参与优化，调制边际对比约束强度。

Result: 在多种细粒度视觉基准上，CV-OPD显著缓解视觉捷径问题，持续提升现有VLM的视觉推理能力；定量与定性实验均验证其有效。

Conclusion: OPD失败源于蒸馏过程缺少视觉对比约束，导致学生利用答案提示走捷径。CV-OPD通过正负裁剪对比与差异感知门，迫使学生依赖视觉证据，从而稳定提升VLM的细粒度视觉推理表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reveals versatility and convenience during VLM post-training, providing dense supervision for student from teacher. However, we observed an anomaly that the distilled VLM could still answer the questions accurately even when the related visual regions were masked or replaced with noise. Building on this observation, our quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that OPD, which uses the answer as a hint, has failed to teach students to genuinely seek out and rely on visual evidence. Our theoretical analysis suggests that this failure arises from a shortcut enabled by the lack of visual constraints during distillation. To address the issue, we introduce Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation (CV-OPD), which compares teacher distributions induced by positive and negative crops while conditioning on the same student prefix and augments optimization with a margin-based contrastive constraint, which is modulated with discrepancy gating across token and sample level. Specifically, CV-OPD first introduces a negative teacher unrelated to the answer and designs a Contrastive Divergence Loss (CDL) that constrains the feature distribution of student by contrasting the positive and negative teachers, correcting how the student exploits visual regions. The CV-OPD introduces a Discrepancy Aware Gate (DAG) that uses token-level and sample-level discrepancies to select visually relevant tokens for optimization, directing the student's attention toward visual content. Extensive experiments across a broad range of fine-grained visual benchmarks demonstrate that CV-OPD substantially alleviates shortcut and consistently improves the visual reasoning capabilities of existing VLMs.

</details>


### [70] [ConClick: Element-Wise Contrastive Learning Improves GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13268)
*Ruotian Zhang,Yuhui Zhang,Junzhe Huang,Yu Xin,Hehe Fan,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出通过对比学习和候选重审来减少GUI定位模型把“干扰项”当成目标的错误，显著提升多基准上的点击定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统评测发现现有GUI grounding模型多数错误不是点到背景，而是反复点到同一类非目标控件（干扰项），导致下游任务失败；因此需要让模型学会区分目标与具迷惑性的干扰项。

Method: 1) 提出ConClick：基于对比学习的框架，构造包含4,292个“目标-干扰项”困难对的ConClick-Data；训练时结合列表式排序(listwise)与成对式(pairwise margin)目标，强化对目标与干扰项的区分。2) 提出ReClick：推理阶段策略，针对高置信候选区域复审，并在竞争候选间作比较，以进一步修正预测。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B骨干上：ScreenSpot-Pro从54.71%升至66.03%，MMBench-GUI L2从83.72%到86.28%，OSWorld-G从63.33%到70.20%；在三大基准上引入ReClick推理后平均准确率再提升2.96个百分点，优于同规模或相近规模的现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过揭示“干扰项点击”这一主要误差模式，并以对比学习与推理复审策略加以针对性改进，方法与数据共同提升了GUI代理的定位可靠性；代码与数据已开源，可作为构建稳健GUI代理的实用工具。

Abstract: GUI grounding, the task of locating the interface element specified by a user instruction, is a fundamental capability of GUI agents. However, existing grounding models remain error-prone, often causing downstream task failures. We systematically evaluate 10 grounding models across five datasets and identify a major error pattern: most incorrect clicks hit non-target interface elements rather than the background, with different models repeatedly selecting the same distractors. Motivated by these findings, we propose ConClick, a contrastive learning framework that teaches models to distinguish the target element from plausible distractors. We construct ConClick-Data, a dataset containing 4,292 challenging target-distractor pairs, and train the model using complementary listwise ranking and pairwise margin objectives. ConClick improves the grounding accuracy of Qwen3-VL-8B from 54.71% to 66.03% on ScreenSpot-Pro, from 83.72% to 86.28% on MMBench-GUI L2, and from 63.33% to 70.20% on OSWorld-G, outperforming prior methods built on the same or similarly sized backbones. We further introduce ReClick, an inference-time strategy that revisits promising regions and compares predictions across competing candidates, yielding an additional 2.96 percentage-point improvement in average accuracy across the three benchmarks. Together, our analysis, dataset, and methods provide both empirical insights and practical tools for building more reliable GUI agents. Data and code are available at https://github.com/zhangruotian/ConClick.

</details>


### [71] [OWLET: On-Policy Weighted Learning via Evidence Transfer for Video Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13272)
*Qitan Lv,Ke Fan,Hong Wang,Wen Wu,Yilin Wang,Bowen Zhou,Feng Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OWLET：一种在视频LLM后训练阶段利用“证据特权视角”的强化学习方法，通过比较两种视频视角（全局均匀+题目相关局部窗 vs 仅均匀采样）来进行证据感知的优势再分配与时空状态对齐，从而更细粒度地利用视觉证据，优于传统RL与OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练范式（RL与OPD）对视频证据利用不足：RL只给出轨迹级奖励，无法区分不同token对视觉证据的依赖程度；OPD虽有token级分布监督，但缺乏对产生这些输出的时空表示的直接约束。实验发现：当视频视角结合全局时序与与问题相关的细节时，同一模型推理更准，提示可从“更富证据的视角”中提炼训练信号。

Method: 构建一个“证据特权视角”（教师视角）：在全局均匀采样的基础上，加入根据问题检索到的局部时空窗口；与仅全局均匀采样（学生/普通视角）对比，得到两视角下的token似然与隐状态差异。基于此提出两项机制：1）证据感知的信用再分配：在存在正的响应级优势时，按照两视角token似然的相对提升为更受证据支持的token分配更高优势；2）时空状态对齐：对经验证器接受的响应，最小化两视角隐藏状态差异，实现对时空表征的直接监督与对齐。

Result: 在广泛实验中，使用证据特权视角（全局+问题相关局部）的训练信号可提升视频推理的准确性；相较传统RL与OPD，OWLET在多项视频理解/推理基准上取得更好的性能，并展示出更好的证据利用与对齐效果（token似然与隐状态更一致地反映相关画面）。

Conclusion: 通过在后训练中引入来自更丰富视觉证据的教师视角，并进行证据感知的优势再分配与时空状态对齐，OWLET细粒度地将响应训练与视觉证据绑定，克服RL过于粗粒度与OPD缺乏表示监督的缺陷，从而显著提升Video-LLM的推理能力。

Abstract: Post-training has become a key stage for eliciting reasoning capabilities in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs). Existing post-training methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, they do not fully exploit visual evidence for effective training. RL supervises the policy with a single trajectory-level reward, which is too coarse-grained for video reasoning: the same response-level advantage is applied to every generated token, disregarding that the tokens of a video response are grounded in the visual stream to very different degrees. OPD provides token-level supervision through output-distribution matching, but lacks direct supervision of the spatiotemporal representations that produce those outputs. We observe across extensive experiments that the same model reasons more accurately when its video view combines global temporal context with question-relevant details. Comparing the same student-generated response under this view and uniform sampling reveals changes in token likelihoods and internal representations, providing a training signal for evidence use. Building on this observation, we propose OWLET, an evidence-privileged RL method that derives training signals from a richer teacher view. This view couples uniform global context with question-conditioned local windows, providing visual evidence without requiring a larger model. To better exploit this privileged visual evidence, OWLET introduces (i) \textbf{evidence-aware credit redistribution}, which compares token likelihoods across the two views and redistributes positive response-level advantages according to their relative evidence support; and (ii) \textbf{spatiotemporal state alignment}, which aligns the two views' hidden states on verifier-accepted responses.

</details>


### [72] [QCLR: A Plug-in Robustification Module via Quantization for Contrastive Learning in Self-Supervised Skeleton-Based Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13281)
*Nanzhu Chen,Feixiang He,Yunfeng Diao,Duygu Sarikaya,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QCLR：在自监督骨架动作识别的对比学习中插入量化鲁棒模块，用双层量化与注意力引导的多面量化，阻断梯度、吸收小扰动，显著提升对白盒/黑盒攻防鲁棒性，同时保持分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督（尤其对比学习）在骨架动作识别上性能优异，但对抗样本易受攻击，实际部署存在安全隐患；需要在不牺牲表征能力/精度的前提下提升鲁棒性，并能无缝接入主流框架。

Method: 在编码流水线中加入可插拔的量化鲁棒化模块QCLR：1）双层量化（dual-level）贯穿编码过程，利用量化桶吸收微小扰动并打断梯度传播，削弱基于梯度的攻击；2）为减轻量化误差，引入注意力引导的多面量化策略，根据特征重要性自适应选择/加权不同量化配置，维持表示质量；与主流对比学习骨架模型无缝集成。

Result: 在多项骨架动作基准上，QCLR集成到主流对比学习模型后，对梯度型白盒与无梯度黑盒攻击的鲁棒性显著提升，同时分类准确率仍与SOTA竞争。

Conclusion: 量化可作为简洁有效的鲁棒化插拔模块：通过双层量化与注意力引导的多面量化，在不显著牺牲表征能力的情况下，显著增强自监督骨架动作对比学习的对抗鲁棒性；方法通用、易集成。

Abstract: Skeleton-based human action recognition has gained increasing attention due to the stability of skeleton data across diverse environments and advances in motion capture technologies. Following the success of supervised learning approaches, self-supervised learning approaches, particularly contrastive learning ones, have achieved impressive performance. However, they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. In this work, we propose a plug-in robustification module via quantization for contrastive learning in self-supervised skeleton-based human action recognition (QCLR), which enhances the robustness of representations against adversarial attacks while preserving their representational capability. Specifically, we employ a dual-level quantization scheme across the encoding pipeline, which breaks the gradient propagation and absorbs small adversarial perturbations within the quantization bins. To mitigate the impact of inevitable quantization error, we further design an attention-guided multi-faceted quantization strategy to maintain the representation quality. Experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that integrating QCLR into a progressive lineage of mainstream contrastive learning models substantially improves robustness against both gradient-based white-box and gradient-free black-box adversarial attacks, while preserving highly competitive classification accuracy.

</details>


### [73] [Decoding Register Tokens Preserves Global Scene Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13299)
*David Jacob Drexlin,Julius Hense,Jonas Dippel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文探究在图像重建与生成中，是否应保留ViT中的非空间“寄存器”token，并通过联合自注意力的解码器和预测器证明这些全局特征能显著降低FID、提升PSNR，相比仅用patch的基线更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流patch解码器忽略ViT的非空间寄存器token，但已有分析显示这些token承载全局属性（照明、模糊、风格）。问题：当已拥有完整patch表示时，保留并利用寄存器特征是否仍能改进重建/生成质量？

Method: 使用冻结、带寄存器token的ViT编码器，构建表示自编码器（RAE）。解码器对“全部patch token+4个寄存器token”做联合自注意力。通过外观探针、重建误差分析与固定解码器的寄存器替换，评测寄存器可达的全局属性及其对像素的影响。生成阶段：训练多头注意力池化（MHAP）预测器，从采样到的patch中回归寄存器特征，不改动预训练扩散模型与其采样。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，ViT-B解码器重建：DINOv2 FID=0.472、PSNR=19.73 dB；DINOv3 FID=0.442、PSNR=20.54 dB；分别较仅patch基线FID下降9.5%与5.2%。生成：基于DINOv2在相同采样patch上，FID=1.641，较基线下降9.8%。

Conclusion: 保留并利用寄存器token能补充全局外观信息，改善重建与生成质量；寄存器对光照、模糊、风格等全局性质具有可访问与可控性。提出的联合注意力解码与MHAP寄存器预测框架，为充分利用ViT非空间表征提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Pretrained vision transformers learn structured image representations spanning spatial patch tokens and non-spatial tokens. Recent analyses suggest that non-spatial register tokens encode global scene properties such as illumination, blur, and style. Yet patch-based image decoders discard these outputs, leaving the decoder to recover image appearance from patches alone. We investigate whether retaining register features improves decoding when the complete spatial patch representation is already available. We study this question using representation autoencoders (RAEs) with frozen register-equipped encoders. Our decoder processes four register tokens together with all patch tokens through joint self-attention. Appearance probes, paired reconstruction-error analysis, and fixed-decoder register substitutions examine which global image properties are accessible from registers and how their values affect reconstructed pixels. For generation, we train a multi-head attention pooling (MHAP) predictor to recover register features from sampled patches, keeping the pretrained diffusion model and its samples unchanged. On ImageNet-1K, our ViT-B decoders achieve mean reconstruction FIDs of 0.472 with DINOv2 and 0.442 with DINOv3, reductions of 9.5% and 5.2% relative to their respective patch-only baselines. The corresponding PSNRs are 19.73 and 20.54 dB. For generation, our DINOv2 models achieve a mean FID of 1.641, 9.8% below the patch-only baseline on identical sampled patches.

</details>


### [74] [Sonic-AMG: Streaming Human Motion Generation from Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13300)
*Jingcheng Ni,Zekun Li,Zhiyang Dou,Xiaoyan Cong,Ziyao Zeng,Hongyang Du,Randall Balestriero,Taku Komura,Srinath Sridhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sonic-AMG提出首个从空间音频流式生成人体反应动作的框架，分离编码“听到什么”和“从哪里来”，用因果Transformer+flow头预测连续动作潜变量，并通过因果解码器重建动作；通过“排练训练”和“空间反馈”缓解训练-推理不匹配，在SAM上取得SOTA并支持实时一致反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法需离线、全序列已知才能生成动作，不符合人类在未知未来声学情境下的在线反应；同时训练时用真值历史与位姿，推理时用生成历史与相对空间，造成分布偏移与一致性问题。

Method: 1) 将声学内容与相对身体的声源几何分离为独立token；2) 因果Transformer带flow head生成连续动作潜变量；3) 因果解码器将潜变量转为动作片段；4) 排练训练：在全序列教师强制下，叠加短自回归自滚动并监督，以缩小历史差异；5) 空间反馈：在训练与推理用生成的root位姿重算相对声源位置，保持空间条件与生成动作一致。

Result: 在SAM数据集上取得更优的FID与R-precision；能够实时在线生成在多声事件与多声源方位下连贯的人体反应。

Conclusion: 通过面向流式的因果建模与针对训练-推理错配的排练训练与空间反馈机制，Sonic-AMG实现了高质量、低延迟且空间一致的人体对空间音频的动作反应生成，并刷新基线表现。

Abstract: Modeling human reactions to spatial audio requires understanding both what is heard and where it comes from. Prior work generates motion offline from complete acoustic and spatial input sequences. Humans, however, react to sounds without knowing what they will hear next. We present Sonic-AMG, to our knowledge the first framework for streaming human motion generation from spatial audio. The model encodes acoustic content and body-relative source geometry as separate tokens. Conditioned on these tokens, a causal transformer with a flow head generates continuous motion latents, from which a causal decoder reconstructs motion segments. Standard teacher-forced training derives motion histories and body-relative source locations from ground-truth motion, whereas streaming inference relies on generated motion for both, resulting in a training-inference mismatch. To reduce this history discrepancy, we introduce rehearsal training, which combines full-sequence teacher-forced supervision with supervised prediction along short autoregressive self-rollouts. To keep spatial conditioning consistent with generated motion, we propose spatial feedback, which recomputes body-relative source locations from generated root poses during both rehearsal training and inference. On SAM, Sonic-AMG achieves state-of-the-art FID and R-precision. It further supports real-time online generation of coherent reactions across sound events and source locations. Project page: https://jingchengni.com/sonic-amg/.

</details>


### [75] [PAGE: Parts-Aware Guidance for Co-Speech Gesture Portrait Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13301)
*Yabiao Wang,Jiafu Wu,Yicheng Xu,Teng Hu,Shengzhe Zhou,Yanxiao Sun,Jiangning Zhang,Ying Tai,Yong Liu,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAGE：用于扩散式语音驱动人像视频生成的“部位感知”指导框架，训练用部位加权监督，推理用一致性分类器与差分指导，显著提升嘴眼手等细节与音视频对齐，并发布大规模中文CAS数据集，达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式语音驱动人像生成虽有较好画质，但局部细节（嘴、眼、手）易退化，且音视频对齐不佳；缺少面向中文场景的大规模评测基准。

Method: 提出PAGE（Parts-Aware GuidancE）框架：1）训练期PSR（部位特定重加权），对嘴、眼、手等语义关键区域自适应加大监督；2）推理期CCG（一致性分类器引导），以语音条件的潜空间分类器检测并校正音视频不一致；3）DG（差分指导），将面部与身体动作分开细化，避免跨区域干扰。框架骨干无关，可插拔用于扩散模型；并构建中文播报员语音CAS数据集。

Result: 在PATS与新建CAS数据集上，PAGE在感知质量、手势多样性、音视频对齐等指标上均达SOTA；CCG与DG在推理中显著减少不一致和细节模糊，PSR提升嘴眼手等关键部位保真度。

Conclusion: 部位感知的训练与推理双层优化能同时提升局部保真与全球对齐；PAGE通用且高效，并以CAS推动中文场景评测，整体达到业界领先表现。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture video generation aims to synthesize realistic, audio-driven portrait videos from a single reference image and a speech signal. Despite advances in visual quality, diffusion-based approaches still suffer from degraded local details and imperfect audio-visual alignment. We present PAGE (Parts-Aware GuidancE), a general, backbone-agnostic optimization framework for diffusion-based generation that enhances local spatial fidelity and global audio-visual consistency at both the training-objective and denoising-trajectory levels. During training, we propose Parts-Specific Reweighting (PSR), which adaptively increases supervision in semantically important regions such as lips, eyes, and hands, improving visual fidelity in perceptually critical areas. During inference, we introduce Consistency Classifier Guidance (CCG), an audio-conditioned latent classifier that identifies and corrects audio-visual inconsistencies in the generated video. We further design Differential Guidance (DG), an efficient inference strategy that refines facial and body motion separately while avoiding cross-region interference. To enable large-scale evaluation, we construct the Chinese Announcer Speech (CAS) dataset, the first comprehensive Chinese benchmark for co-speech gesture portrait video generation. Compared with the widely used PATS dataset, CAS includes substantially more identities, video clips, and scene diversity. Extensive experiments on both PATS and CAS demonstrate that PAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance in perceptual quality, gesture diversity, and audio-visual alignment. Project page: https://aigc-explorer.github.io/PAGE/.

</details>


### [76] [NovaSplat: Model-Free 6D Object Pose Estimation via 3D Gaussian Splatting Onboarding and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13309)
*Georgios Floros,Evangelos Sartinas,Orestis Vangelis,Maria Pateraki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NovaSplat是一种无需CAD模型的新物体6D位姿估计管线：通过入库时的一段手机视频训练3D Gaussian Splatting模型，既用于检测与粗位姿估计，也作为可微骨干做位姿细化；提出无奇异的连续旋转参数化，结合光度与几何损失优化位姿；在家居数据集上超越所有基于模型的方法，在工业（无纹理/高反）数据集上显著领先无模型方法并缩小与基于模型方法的差距；另有推理更快、精度几乎不损的NovaSplat-Lite（3.1×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模型（model-free）6D位姿估计需要在入库时从零构建物体表示，精度与鲁棒性长期落后于依赖准确CAD的基于模型方法，尤其在纹理少、镜面等工业场景更困难；需要一种既不依赖CAD又能提供高质量几何与外观先验并可用于可微优化的表示与流程。

Method: - 入库阶段：用短手机视频训练3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）模型，获得物体的稠密外观与几何表示。
- 检测与粗配准：利用3DGS作为模板源进行目标检测与初始6D位姿估计。
- 可微细化：以3DGS为可微渲染骨干，联合光度（渲染-观测一致）与几何损失优化物-相机位姿。
- 旋转表示：采用无梯度奇异性的连续旋转参数化以稳定优化。
- 变体：NovaSplat-Lite在推理时作效率优化，实现3.1×速度提升，精度几乎不降。

Result: - 家居物体基准：性能超过所有基于模型的方法。
- 工业基准（纹理少、镜面）：优于所有无模型方法，并大幅缩小与基于模型方法的差距。
- 推理：NovaSplat-Lite实现约3.1×加速且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 3DGS可作为无模型6D位姿估计的强大统一表示：既能提供模板用于检测与粗估计，也能作为可微渲染器进行高精度位姿优化；配合稳定的旋转参数化与联合光度/几何损失，NovaSplat在多场景下达到或逼近基于模型方法的性能，并提供高效推理变体。

Abstract: 6D pose estimation of novel objects without CAD models remains an open challenge: model-free methods must build the object representation from scratch during onboarding and have long trailed their model-based counterparts. We present NovaSplat, a model-free pipeline that closes this gap. From a short smartphone video captured at onboarding, we train a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model that serves both as a template source for detection and coarse pose estimation and as a differentiable backbone for pose refinement. Our refiner optimizes the object-to-camera pose with combined photometric and geometric losses under a continuous rotation parametrization free of gradient singularities. On a household-object benchmark, NovaSplat surpasses all model-based methods. On a challenging industrial benchmark with textureless and specular surfaces, it outperforms all model-free competitors while substantially narrowing the gap to model-based ones. We further introduce NovaSplat-Lite, an inference variant that is $3.1\times$ faster at negligible accuracy cost. Code will be made available at \href{https://github.com/POSE-Lab/novasplat}{https://github.com/POSE-Lab/novasplat}.

</details>


### [77] [ThinkDrag: Semantic Drag-Based Image Editing with Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13316)
*Armando Fortes,Tianyi Wei,Chindanai Trakantannarong,Shangchen Zhou,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThinkDrag 是一个将“拖拽式”图像编辑与视觉推理结合的统一多模态框架：不只把拖拽当作几何位移，而是结合内容理解与语义变换来生成更合理的编辑结果；并可输出显式推理轨迹与端点标记，达到更高质量、可解释且精确的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拖拽编辑方法多将用户的手柄/目标点视为纯几何位移信号，难以在语义歧义或需内容理解的场景中产出合理变换；缺乏对拖拽与对象/场景级语义变换的关联与可解释性评估基准。

Method: 提出 ThinkDrag：一个统一多模态框架，将点约束与视觉推理结合，训练模型学习从稀疏点到对象/场景级语义变换的映射；可选地生成显式“推理轨迹”来先解释编辑意图再进行图像生成；引入“拖拽端点标记”（受跨图注意力分析启发）以在图像空间中提供清晰对应信号；构建语义拖拽+推理标注的监督数据集，并发布用于复杂场景的 DragBench++ 基准。

Result: 在多项实验中达成 SOTA：生成质量与语义合理性显著提升，同时保持与现有方法相当的点跟随精度；在歧义场景中具备更好的可解释性。

Conclusion: 语义推理与显式对应信号能显著增强拖拽式图像编辑的可控性、合理性与可解释性；ThinkDrag 与 DragBench++ 为未来研究提供了数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Drag-based image editing provides an intuitive interface for spatially controlled image manipulation by allowing users to specify handle and target points. Existing methods have made substantial progress through optimization-based, one-step, and feed-forward formulations, but they often interpret drag instructions as geometric displacements. This limits their effectiveness when the desired edit depends on interpreting image content and inferring a plausible semantic transformation from sparse point constraints. We introduce ThinkDrag, a unified multimodal framework for drag-based image editing with visual reasoning. ThinkDrag is trained to associate point constraints with meaningful object- and scene-level transformations, and can optionally produce an explicit reasoning trace that interprets the intended edit before image generation. This reasoning trace improves interpretability, particularly for ambiguous drags. Drag Endpoint Markers, motivated by an analysis of cross-image attention, provide an explicit correspondence signal that grounds each drag in image space. To support this framework, we construct a supervised dataset of semantic drag transformations paired with reasoning traces and introduce DragBench++, a benchmark targeting challenging drag-based editing scenarios with reference edit solutions. Experiments show that ThinkDrag achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving generation quality and plausibility while maintaining competitive point-following precision.

</details>


### [78] [CoVA: Composing VAE Representations for Generative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13322)
*Zhaowei Chen,Yikai Ge,Linze Li,Yao Tang,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoVA，通过在线组合VAE特征为flow-matching的SiT训练提供阶段式指导，8.5M训练期参数、无推理开销；在ImageNet256上大幅降FID并加速收敛，同时纠正软标签条件中的幅度衰减混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用外部视觉编码器对齐可改善扩散Transformer训练，但需额外编码器和开销；直接用VAE特征又偏重重建，欠缺语义判别与空间结构。需要一种轻量方法在不增加推理成本下，获得更语义、具空间感的特征用于生成模型训练，并澄清软标签条件中的潜在混淆因素。

Method: 提出CoVA：在训练过程中用一个小型“latent composer”在线组合VAE潜特征，并以三种损失联合训练：类别监督、掩膜潜空间重建、水平翻转一致性，从而得到语义判别且空间稠密的特征和样本依赖的类别后验。训练前期用这些组合表征对齐SiT中间特征，随后移除对齐以避免负效应，同时将composer的后验（分离梯度）作为样本依赖软标签进行条件化。发现并分析软标签条件中的幅度衰减混淆：平均类别嵌入会降低向量模长，导致“语义插值”与“条件强度”耦合。CoVA通过恢复硬标签的模长来解耦，仅保留后验方向上的语义。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，SiT-XL/2的FID从17.2降至7.4（400K步，降幅57%）。结合无分类器引导时，400个epoch达到FID 2.03，而原生SiT-XL/2需1400个epoch才能到2.06。仅增加8.5M训练期参数（占SiT-XL/2的1.3%），推理期零开销。

Conclusion: CoVA通过可学习的VAE特征组合与阶段式对齐显著提升SiT的样本质量与收敛速度，并揭示并修正软标签条件中的模长衰减混淆，最终以极小的额外训练成本取得SOTA级别的FID和训练效率。

Abstract: Representation alignment with pretrained visual encoders improves diffusion transformer training but requires auxiliary encoders and extra training overhead. Recent approaches instead exploit representations from the generative model's own variational autoencoder (VAE). However, reconstruction-oriented VAE features are not optimized for the semantic discrimination and spatial structure needed for generative learning. We introduce \textbf{CoVA} (\textbf{Co}mposed \textbf{VA}E Representations), a lightweight framework that composes VAE features online into stage-wise guidance for flow-matching training. A small latent composer is jointly trained with class supervision, masked latent reconstruction, and horizontal-flip consistency, yielding semantically discriminative, spatially structured dense features and sample-dependent class posteriors. CoVA aligns intermediate SiT features with the composed representations early in training, then removes the increasingly detrimental alignment and reuses detached composer posteriors for sample-dependent soft label conditioning. We further uncover a hidden confound in soft label conditioning: averaging class embeddings attenuates their magnitude, coupling semantic interpolation with conditioning strength. This attenuation alone can substantially improve FID while degrading Recall, revealing a non-semantic source of the apparent gain. CoVA corrects this confound by restoring the hard-label magnitude, isolating posterior-dependent directional semantics. CoVA adds only 8.5M training-only auxiliary parameters (1.3\% of SiT-XL/2) and introduces no inference-time overhead. On ImageNet 256$\times$256, CoVA reduces SiT-XL/2 FID from 17.2 to 7.4 (\textbf{57\%}) in 400K steps. With classifier-free guidance, it achieves an FID of \textbf{2.03} in only 400 epochs, versus 2.06 after 1,400 epochs for vanilla SiT-XL/2.

</details>


### [79] [LETHE: Defending against Unauthorized Image-to-Video Generation via Value Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13333)
*Boheng Li,Yueming Xing,Yiming Li,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LETHE：通过收缩参考图像在I2V扩散模型中的“value向量”分布并吸引后续帧关注这些塌缩token，从而破坏视频一致性与保真度，达到对滥用的对抗性保护；在新建的人像基准与多模型上达SOTA防护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I2V扩散模型可将单张人物照变为高质量、主体一致的视频，带来隐私与滥用风险。现有在图像上添加对抗扰动的保护方法在常见扰动预算下效果有限，根因是对现代I2V中“后续帧如何读取参考图像”的机制理解不足。

Method: 从理论与实证两方面解析I2V的读取机制：通过凸几何分析，识别“参考value向量的分布扩散度（spread）”是关键因子；发现轻微收缩该spread即可显著劣化生成视频。基于此提出LETHE：1) Value Collapse目标使参考value向量趋于一致（塌缩）；2) Attention Attraction目标将后续帧的注意力吸引到这些塌缩token上，放大失真。并构建两个人体相关I2V防护基准进行评测。

Result: 在多种现代I2V模型与两个人像基准上，LETHE实现最先进的防护：在常规扰动预算内显著降低视频保真度与主体一致性，且具有通用性与高效性。

Conclusion: 控制参考图像在I2V中被“读取”的value向量spread是高效防护的关键杠杆。通过value塌缩与注意力吸引的联合目标，LETHE能在不增加扰动预算的情况下显著破坏I2V生成效果，提供更强大且通用的对抗性保护。

Abstract: Image-to-video (I2V) diffusion models can animate a single reference image into high-quality, subject-consistent videos, posing threats of misuse as anyone can turn personal photos into fabricated videos without consent. A promising solution is to proactively add an adversarial perturbation to the image before release, such that videos generated from it would be unfaithful or visually corrupted. However, due to an insufficient understanding of what governs how subsequent frames read the reference image in modern I2V models, existing protections struggle to offer strong protection under common perturbation budgets. In this paper, we approach the problem by exploring this underlying reading mechanism. Through a convex geometric analysis, we theoretically identify the spread of the reference value vectors as a key factor governing this reading process, and empirically find that even a mild contraction of this spread can severely degrade the generated video, making it an efficient lever for protection. Guided by this insight, we propose LETHE, a novel protection method that directly disrupts this reading process. LETHE pairs two complementary objectives, where a value collapse objective drives the reference value vectors toward uniformity, while an attention attraction objective draws subsequent frames toward these collapsed tokens to amplify corruption. We further construct two human-centric I2V protection benchmarks and conduct extensive experiments with multiple modern I2V models, which demonstrate the state-of-the-art protection performance and versatility of LETHE.

</details>


### [80] [Exploring Attention Sinks in Text-to-Image Transformers from Diffusion and Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13337)
*Binglei Li,Mengping Yang,Zhiyu Tan,Wenxu Qian,Junping Zhang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究了文本到图像Transformer中的“注意力汇”（attention sinks），发现它们在扩散式双向模型与自回归模型中均出现，但分布与作用不同；通过分析与干预显示，汇相关的头和位置对生成影响显著，并可用寄存器token重分配注意力，部分配置还能保持或提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NLP大模型中注意力汇现象已被广泛讨论，但在文本到图像Transformer（T2I）中的存在性、分布规律与功能影响尚不清楚。理解这些机制有助于解释与改进T2I模型的注意力路由与生成质量。

Method: 对比两类T2I Transformer：双向扩散模型与因果自回归模型。1）系统性注意力分析：跨层、跨头、跨阶段统计注意力质量，定位稳定吸收注意力的token/位置；2）有针对性的干预：扰动与屏蔽被判定为“汇”的注意力头与token位置，测量对生成分数与输出的影响；3）引入寄存器（register）token以吸纳或重分配注意力，评估对性能的影响。

Result: 两类模型都出现注意力汇但模式不同：自回归模型偏向首token与全局尺度token；双向扩散模型在模态边界、文本区域内部与图像空间角落形成注意力汇。该现象随生成阶段变化，且集中于少量层与头。扰动“汇”排名较高的注意力头比非汇头引发更大平均变化；对部分“汇”位置的扰动在双向模型上对评分影响有限。引入寄存器token可重新分配注意力，若干配置能保持或小幅提升生成表现。

Conclusion: 注意力汇在T2I Transformer中普遍存在且依赖架构、推理阶段与具体组件。识别与调控这些汇有助于开展机理分析，并为设计更有效的注意力机制与稳健的生成流程提供原则性指导。

Abstract: Attention sinks are tokens or positions that consistently receive disproportionate attention mass, often beyond what their semantic content would suggest. Despite extensive study in large language models, their behavior and functional relevance in text-to-image (T2I) Transformers remain largely unexplored. To fill this gap and better understand how text-to-image Transformers allocate and route attention, we explore this phenomenon of text-to-image transformers across bidirectional diffusion models and causal autoregressive models. Specifically, we explore whether attention sinks exist, where they emerge, and how they functionally influence generation. Through systematic attention analysis and targeted interventions, we identify that attention sinks emerge in both bidirectional diffusion and autoregressive models but exhibit distinct patterns. The autoregressive model concentrates attention on the first and global-scale tokens, whereas the bidirectional models develop sinks near modality boundaries, within text regions, and at spatial corners. Moreover, these patterns vary across generation stages and are localized to a sparse subset of layers and heads. To assess their functional relevance, we perturb sink-related heads and token positions. It turns out that perturbing sink-ranked heads produces larger average changes than non-sink comparisons, while some sink-position perturbations have limited effects on bidirectional model scores. We further introduce register tokens to redistribute attention sinks and find that several configurations preserve or modestly improve generation performance. Overall, attention sinks are architecture-, inference-stage-, and component-dependent phenomena, providing a basis for mechanistic analysis and principled attention design in text-to-image transformers.

</details>


### [81] [VideoAWR: Reinforcing Video Flow Models with Decoupled-Timestep Advantage-Weighted Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13338)
*Zheming Fu,Wenbin Ning,Ruizhe He,Wei Shang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Lianrui Mu,Chang Liu,Lei Wang,Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoAWR：在视频流模型上用回归式强化学习，解耦采样与优化锚点，用指导采样稳定奖励分离、用无指导锚点做校正；理论证明目标精确、避免CFG爆炸，并引入解耦时间步校正以提效；在Wan2.1-1.3B/14B上实现稳定优化与更优画质与运动，在无CFG与有CFG两种推理下皆提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 把基于回归的RL扩展到视频扩散/流模型时，必须无CFG采样以与参考边际速度对齐；但无指导采样在视频场景易陷低保真区，奖励差异失真导致AWR目标失去意义；若改用有指导采样又会在EMA下引发几何级数式的指导强度膨胀。需要一种既能恢复高保真与奖励可分性，又不引起指导失稳的训练范式。

Method: 提出VideoAWR：- 训练—采样解耦：用EMA参考策略进行有指导（guided）rollout以恢复画质与奖励可分性；但训练的回归目标锚定在无指导（unguided）条件分布。- 理论：中心化advantage在期望下抵消锚点，仅保留与奖励相关的协方差增量，过滤与奖励无关的CFG偏移，从而目标“数学上精确”。- 引入Decoupled Timestep Rectification：对每次rollout随机独立抽样时间步并监督，减少偏置、提升样本效率。- 训练稳定于EMA更新，无需在目标中绑定指导项，避免CFG几何爆炸。

Result: 在Wan2.1的1.3B与14B视频流模型上，训练稳定，生成的视觉与运动质量优于基线；在CFG-free与启用CFG的推理两种设置下均提升，且推理默认单分支、无CFG即可表现良好，同时在需要时仍可使用CFG且语义未被破坏。

Conclusion: 通过解耦“采样用于收集数据”和“优化锚点用于回归”的两角色，VideoAWR恢复奖励可分性并避免CFG累积失稳；其目标具有严格理论支撑，并配合解耦时间步校正提升效率，最终在不同模型规模与推理设定下实现更高质量与稳定性。

Abstract: Regression-based RL for flow models mandates sampling \emph{without} classifier-free guidance (CFG) to align the rectification target with the reference marginal velocity. Extending Advantage-Weighted Rectification (AWR), which dynamically scales this regression target based on reward, to video generation encounters a cold-start pathology: unguided rollouts collapse into low-fidelity regions where intra-group reward gaps remain wide but no longer track perceptual quality, rendering the optimization target arbitrary. Conversely, utilizing guided rollouts to restore fidelity, while keeping the regression target tied to the guided sampling distribution, provably triggers a geometric guidance blow-up under exponential-moving-average (EMA) updates. We propose \textbf{VideoAWR}, which resolves this tension by untying sampling from the optimization anchor. An EMA reference policy generates \emph{guided} rollouts to restore reward separability, while the trainable policy is rectified against the \emph{unguided} conditional anchor. We prove this decoupled objective is mathematically exact: the centered advantage cancels the anchor in expectation, filtering out the reward-neutral guidance offset and retaining only the reward-correlated covariance increment. Consequently, inference runs single-branch and CFG-free, yet inference-time CFG preserves its original semantics. Furthermore, we introduce \emph{Decoupled Timestep Rectification}, supervising each rollout at an independently sampled timestep without bias to improve sample efficiency. Experiments on Wan2.1-1.3B and 14B demonstrate stable optimization and superior visual and motion quality in \emph{both} CFG-free and CFG-enabled regimes. Code is available at \pdfstartlink attr{/Border [0 0 0]} user{/Subtype /Link /A << /S /URI /URI (https://github.com/Fuzm7/FlowAWR) >>}% \textcolor{pearDark}{VideoAWR}\pdfendlink.

</details>


### [82] [Why DiT Models Underperform as Representation Learners without Long Skip Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13344)
*Benyuan Meng,Yiliang Zhang,Jin-Wen Wu,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang,Longtao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文探讨在Diffusion Transformer（DiT）中加入长跳连（LSC）以改善判别特征质量：LSC把扩散噪声信号直连到需要的位置，减弱主干内的噪声污染，从而提升中间特征的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散特征可统一生成与理解，但DiT尽管生成强，判别特征弱，影响统一。作者怀疑主因在于噪声与图像信号在主干混合传播，导致表征被噪声污染，需要一种结构性改进。

Method: 从零开始受控训练对比带/不带长跳连（LSC）的DiT；并做诊断分析：将扩散输入分解为潜在图像与扩散噪声两路，考察噪声经主干传播对特征的影响；使用LSC将噪声直接旁路到预测头或相关层，减小主干中的噪声幅度。

Result: 带LSC的DiT生成能力不减且中间特征在判别任务上明显更强；实验证明特征退化主要来自噪声在主干的“污染”，LSC有效缓解。

Conclusion: LSC并非技巧，而与扩散目标密切相关：扩散骨干需同时获取图像与噪声信息，最佳路径是让噪声经旁路直达需要处，降低对图像特征通路的干扰，从而实现更好的生成-理解统一。

Abstract: Diffusion feature, i.e., intermediate activations extracted from diffusion model backbones, has emerged as a promising approach for unifying generation and vision understanding (discrimination). Despite the advancement of diffusion backbones into Diffusion Transformers (DiT), it has been found in previous literature that DiT models tend to yield weaker features despite their stronger generation capability. This poses a threat to good generation-understanding unification. In this work, we show via controlled from-scratch training that long skip connections (LSCs) affect feature quality: a DiT with LSCs can produce better features. Furthermore, through detailed diagnosis, we reveal the mechanism causing this effect. Specifically, the input to a diffusion backbone contains both the latent image and the diffusion noise, where the diffusion noise, though appearing in the form of noises, actually reflects the information of the diffusion trajectory. To make the final prediction of a noisy variable ($v$ or $ε$), the diffusion backbone needs both signals. However, carrying both signals through the main backbone effectively "pollutes" the image signal with noises, leading to poor feature quality. In comparison, LSCs can shortcut diffusion noises directly to where they are needed, leaving much weaker noise signals in the main backbone, thus improving feature quality. To name the broader impact, this reveals that LSCs are not a mere trick but closely related to the diffusion objective. The code is at https://github.com/Darkbblue/dit-lsc.

</details>


### [83] [TextVBench: Evaluating Text Rendering and Preservation in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13412)
*Ruiqiang Zhang,Hengyi Wang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Wang,Zehua Ma,Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextVBench提出面向视频中文字评测的多目标、多实例时空对应框架，含T2V与Ref2V两轨，采用目标条件候选选择，分解空间与时间一致性，并以长度加权调和分数总结表现，提供诊断切片揭示失败模式；实测显示随参考文本区域数增加性能明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器常出现文字渲染错误、缺字、跨帧变化等问题，且多目标、多噪声OCR场景下简单逐帧OCR难以客观评估。需要一个能在复杂时空对应下准确衡量“生成/保持文字”的基准与指标。

Method: 将视觉-文本评测表述为多目标、多实例的时空对应问题：1) 两条评测轨道——T2V评测按提示生成与维持文字，Ref2V评测从参考图保持文字；2) 采用“目标条件的候选选择”而非无约束VLM转写；3) 允许一目标映射到多个OCR区域；4) 将帧级空间忠实度与跨帧时间一致性解耦评估；5) 用长度加权调和分数汇总，并提供额外诊断维度；6) 数据按文本、版式、载体与运动等受控属性组织，便于溯源能力边界。

Result: 在评测的模型中，MiniMax-H3在T2V上的TextVScore为68.32；Ref2V最佳得分为56.51。按属性切片显示：当同时需要保持的参考文本区域数量增加时，性能明显下降。

Conclusion: TextVBench为视频中文字评测提供了系统化、可解释的基准与指标体系，能分离空间与时间维度、处理多目标多实例对应，并通过诊断切片定位失败模式；当前模型在多参考文本场景下仍存在显著瓶颈。

Abstract: Modern video generators can synthesize convincing scenes while rendering a few characters incorrectly, omitting requested text, or changing it across frames. Measuring these failures is harder than applying OCR independently to each frame: when a scene contains multiple text targets and multiple noisy OCR regions, the evaluator must first determine which observation belongs to which intended text. We introduce TextVBench, a benchmark and evaluation framework that formulates visual-text evaluation as a multi-target, multi-instance spatiotemporal correspondence problem. TextVBench contains two complementary tracks: TextVBench-T2V measures the creation and maintenance of prompt-specified text, while TextVBench-Ref2V measures preservation of text from reference images. Both tracks use target-conditioned candidate selection rather than unconstrained VLM transcription, support one target mapping to multiple OCR regions, and separate frame-level spatial fidelity from cross-frame temporal fidelity. A length-weighted harmonic score summarizes these factors, while extra-text diagnostics expose failure modes hidden by a single OCR score. The benchmark is organized around controlled text, layout, carrier, and motion attributes so that aggregate scores can be traced back to specific capability limits. Across the evaluated model runs, MiniMax-H3 reaches a T2V TextVScore of 68.32, while the strongest Ref2V score is 56.51; attribute slices reveal a pronounced drop as the number of simultaneous reference text regions increases.

</details>


### [84] [SADL: A Size-Adaptive Decision Layer for Chronic Stroke Lesion Segmentation in ISLES 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13422)
*Marek Wodzinski,Gniewosz Drwiega,Wojciech Szymanski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种针对T1加权MRI中慢性缺血性卒中病灶的难分割问题的集成分割与决策框架。基于11个nnU-Net变体的加权软融合，并配套自适应决策层SADL，实现对多尺度病灶的稳健检测与阈值自适应，显著提升小病灶检测与总体指标，在站点隔离的91例验证集上取得Dice 0.732、lesion-F1 0.746、PR-AUC 0.843，并以非参数自举显著性验证优于单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性缺血性卒中病灶在T1像上与正常组织外观重叠、体积跨度大（四个数量级），导致体素级与病灶级评价常不一致，固定阈值与单一模型难以兼顾小病灶灵敏度与总体精度。因此需要一种既能融合多模型优势、又能针对不同病灶尺度自适应决策的方案，同时避免调参与测试集泄漏。

Method: 构建11成员的nnU-Net集成，覆盖五个设计轴：1) 基线Dice+Top-K损失；2) 重数据增强；3) 按病灶体积加权的损失重加权；4) 随机权重平均（SWA）；5) 采样与结构联合块：队列均衡采样+残差编码器主干。将成员概率图进行加权软融合，输入到调优的SADL决策层：估计病灶体积、根据病灶大小自适应阈值、按体素数与峰值概率裁剪连通域，并加入“永不为空”的救援策略。融合权重与分桶参数均基于OOF预测拟合，避免使用测试集信息。

Result: 在与训练站点不重叠的91例留出集上，在线评分获得Dice 0.7322、lesion-F1 0.7461、PR-AUC 0.8430。与单一集成成员相比，配对非参数自举显示lesion-F1提升显著（p=0.024），提升主要体现在小病灶场景，救援步骤能挽回固定阈值会漏检的病例。

Conclusion: 多轴多样性的nnU-Net集成配合SADL自适应决策，有效缓解T1上慢性卒中病灶分割的尺度与外观挑战，在病灶级指标尤其是小病灶检测上显著优于单模型。通过OOF拟合避免测试集泄漏，结果具有更高可信度，可为临床与挑战赛场景提供稳健方案。

Abstract: Chronic ischemic stroke lesions are difficult to segment on T1-weighted MRI. Their appearance overlaps with healthy tissue, and their volumes span four orders of magnitude, so voxel-level and lesion level metrics often disagree. We build an eleven-member nnU-Net ensemble that covers five design axes: a baseline dice + top-K loss, heavy augmentation, per-lesion volume loss re-weighting, stochastic weight averaging, and a sampling-and-architecture block that pairs cohort-balanced sampling with a residual-encoder backbone. Weighted soft-map fusion of the members feeds a tuned decision layer, SADL, that estimates lesion volume, applies an adaptive per-lesion-size threshold, prunes components by voxel count and peak probability, and adds a never-empty rescue. All fusion weights and per-bucket parameters are fitted on out-of-fold (OOF) predictions, so tuning never touches the held-out test set. On a site-disjoint 91-case holdout, SADL reaches Dice 0.7322, lesion-F1 0.7461 and PR-AUC 0.8430 when scored online with the challenge's released evaluation code. A paired non-parametric bootstrap against a reference single ensemble member confirms the lesion-F1 gain at p=0.024, and the gain is largest in the small-lesion regime, where the rescue step recovers cases that a fixed threshold would silently discard.

</details>


### [85] [When MLLMs miss what humans read: diagnosing and learning to read squint text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13465)
*Chen Peng,Shan Huang,Jianbo Huang,Linrui Qin,Qibo Sun,Mengwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“Squint Text”概念，指出多模态大模型在图像粗粒度结构中隐含文本的识读缺陷，并构建真实与合成数据集进行诊断。发现瓶颈在空间选择与高频干扰，提出以冻结教师分布蒸馏的Squint OPSD，在仅原图推理下显著提升读字性能（ANLS 88.61 vs 26.50基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内容审核等实际场景中，图像里存在人类“眯眼”即可感知的粗粒度文本，但多模态LLM常误报无字或读不准，暴露出模型在低分辨率/粗结构文本上的识读缺陷。为定位机制性原因并在不增加推理时辅助视图的前提下提升读字能力，作者系统研究这一问题。

Method: 1) 定义问题并构建两套基准：SquintBench（1076条来自生产审核的样本）与可控合成套件SquintCtrl用于机制诊断。2) 冻结权重分析：探查隐藏表示对文本存在与字形身份的编码，以及提供正确空间区域时的可读性变化；评估视觉token预算与高频抑制的影响。3) 提出Squint OPSD：给冻结教师一个下采样视图和真实转写作为特权上下文，获取其token级分布；学生模型沿on-policy轨迹蒸馏学习。推理时仅用原始图像，无需辅助视图或转写。

Result: 关键发现：- 文本存在的粗信号可被编码，但具体字形身份表示弱；给出正确空间位置后身份显著更可及，说明空间选择是主要瓶颈。- 抑制高频内容与控制视觉token数可提升读字。性能：在SquintBench上，Squint OPSD达88.61 ANLS，显著优于未适配骨干26.50、原始视图下的最佳通用模型24.44，以及仅看下采样视图的现成模型最高68.60。

Conclusion: “眯眼文本”揭示多模态LLM在空间选择与频率处理上的系统性缺陷。通过特权教师分布蒸馏，学生在不依赖推理时辅助视图的前提下，大幅缩小“可用视觉证据”与“生成读出”之间的差距，为实际审核与鲁棒OCR式读字提供可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs can fail to read text embedded in an image's coarse structure (text a human recovers by squinting) and report that no text is present. We name this class Squint Text and study it as a recognition deficiency, motivated by cases from a production moderation pipeline. We introduce SquintBench, comprising 1076 production-derived moderation images, and SquintCtrl, a controlled synthetic suite for mechanism diagnosis. Frozen-weight analysis shows that hidden text leaves a coarse signal while glyph identity is weakly represented; identity becomes substantially more accessible when the correct spatial regions are supplied, implicating spatial selection as a major bottleneck. Suppressing high-frequency content also improves reading at a fixed visual-token budget, while visual token count may also contribute. These findings motivate Squint OPSD: a frozen teacher receives a downscaled view and the ground-truth transcription as privileged context, and its token-level distributions supervise a student along on-policy trajectories. At inference, the student reads the original image alone. Squint OPSD reaches 88.61 ANLS on SquintBench, versus 26.50 for the unadapted backbone, 24.44 for the best general-purpose model on the original view, and 68.60 at best for an off-the-shelf model under a downscaled view. These results substantially narrow the gap between available visual evidence and its generated read-out without auxiliary views at inference.

</details>


### [86] [A Real-World CAD Segmentation Benchmark: Comparing Rule-Based and Learning-Based Approaches through Hole Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13466)
*Kai Schütte,Edgar Heinert,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估用于工程CAD中紧固件相关孔检测的多种方法，并发布人工标注基准数据集，系统比较基于规则与基于学习方法在准确率、召回率、数据需求与域泛化方面的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD分割数据多为合成或自动标注，难以代表真实工程任务；孔检测既需人工标注又具更高几何复杂度，缺乏能检验方法可迁移性的真实数据与系统评测。

Method: 1) 提出两种任务特定的基于规则方法：图-线搜索（graph-wire-search）与多视图法；并构建来自工程CAD的人为标注基准，可用于训练UV-Net与AAGNet等B-Rep分割学习模型。2) 进行广泛实验：参数优化；在新基准上评估；在合成域做跨域泛化；分析不同数据规模与IoU阈值下的可行性。3) 对比分析不同方法性能与鲁棒性。

Result: 基于规则方法无需训练数据即可良好泛化：图-线搜索更偏向高召回，多视图更偏向高精度；基于学习方法在标注充足时表现强，但在域迁移下鲁棒性下降。

Conclusion: 真实工程孔检测与更广泛CAD分割中，存在数据需求与鲁棒性的权衡：规则法更稳健、无需数据但各有偏好（召回vs精度）；学习法在有足够标注时最佳但对域偏移敏感。该基准与分析为方法选择与实际部署提供指导。

Abstract: CAD segmentation datasets are predominantly synthetic or automatically labelled, leaving it unclear how well existing methods transfer to real-world engineering tasks. Fastener-relevant hole detection represents one such task, requiring human annotation and exhibiting greater geometric complexity than commonly used benchmarks. We address this gap through three main contributions. First, we develop two task-specific rule-based methods, one graph-wire-search and one multi-view based, and complement them with a human-annotated benchmark dataset derived from engineering CAD data that also enables training of UV-Net and AAGNet, representative learning-based approaches for B-Rep segmentation. Second, we conduct an extensive experimental study including parameter optimisation, evaluation on our benchmark dataset, cross-domain generalisation on a synthetic dataset, and an assessment of the feasibility of the approaches at varying dataset sizes and IoU thresholds. Third, we provide evidence-based insights into the strengths and limitations of different approaches: rule-based methods generalise well without training data, with the graph-wire-search method favouring recall and the multi-view method favouring precision, whereas learning-based methods achieve strong performance with sufficient annotations but exhibit reduced robustness under domain shift. These findings highlight trade-offs between data requirements, and robustness, and provide guidance not only for hole detection, but more broadly for real-world CAD segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [87] [GraftOOD: Grafting Out-of-Distribution Exemplars into Advantage-Weighted Rectification for Video Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13470)
*Zheming Fu,Ruizhe He,Wenbin Ning,Xiaoxiao Ma,Xuan Yang,Lianrui Mu,Haoyang Huang,Chang Liu,Lei Wang,Siming Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GraftOOD，通过在部分提示组中用分布外视频样本替换部分在线rollout，并结合共享速度回归目标，缓解视频奖励优化导致的“运动坍塌”（动态减少但代理奖励升高）。在Wan2.1-1.3B上显著提升Dynamic Degree至44.41，相比基线FlowAWR的4.86，同时保持VisionReward稳定、HPSv3略降。构建GraftSet-Video（9,588示例/2,397提示）提供外部监督。消融显示增加样本频率并非单调收益，存在动态-奖励权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用标量奖励优化视频生成会出现“运动坍塌”：代理奖励升高却视频动态性下降，哪怕奖励与整体运动质量相关。需要一种既能对齐奖励、又能保持/提升视频动态的训练策略，且不依赖额外监督权重或可获得的示例生成轨迹。

Method: 提出GraftOOD：在选定的提示组内，用来自外部分布（GraftSet-Video）的示例视频替换部分在线rollout；并引入共享的速度回归目标，将“组内相对奖励校正”与“序列级示例监督”结合，无需示例的生成轨迹与额外监督系数。理论上，其点对点最优场是源后验加权的两个源目标场的组合。

Result: 构建GraftSet-Video数据集（9,588个与2,397个提示格式匹配的示例）。在Wan2.1-1.3B上，相对仅奖励的FlowAWR：Dynamic Degree从4.86→44.41，HPSv3从8.10→7.32，VisionReward基本不变。与三种示例增强方法比较，在主设定下GraftOOD在10项指标中领先9项。消融表明示例频率与组内示例数量提高并非单调收益，呈现动态—奖励的权衡。

Conclusion: 外部示例监督可有效补足标量奖励反馈，缓解运动坍塌并显著提升视频动态，但更高的动态度不必然等同于连贯、可信的运动；需要在动态与总体感知质量之间权衡。

Abstract: Video reward optimization can increase proxy rewards while reducing video dynamics, a mismatch we identify as \emph{motion collapse}, including under a video-level motion-quality reward. We propose \textbf{GraftOOD}, which replaces part of the online rollouts in selected prompt groups with out-of-distribution video exemplars. A shared velocity-regression objective combines group-relative reward rectification with sequence-level exemplar supervision, requiring neither exemplar generation trajectories nor an additional supervised-loss coefficient. We characterize its pointwise optimal field as a source-posterior-weighted combination of the two source-specific target fields. We construct \textbf{GraftSet-Video}, containing 9,588 format-matched exemplars for 2,397 prompts, to supply external supervision. On Wan2.1-1.3B, GraftOOD raises Dynamic Degree from 4.86 to 44.41 relative to reward-only FlowAWR, while HPSv3 decreases from 8.10 to 7.32 and VisionReward remains close. At the main setting, GraftOOD leads nine of ten metrics among three exemplar-augmented methods. Ablations of exemplar frequency and within-group count reveal dynamics--reward trade-offs rather than uniform gains from increasing exemplar usage. These results support external exemplar supervision as a complement to scalar reward feedback, without equating higher measured dynamics with coherent, visually plausible motion in the generated videos. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Fuzm7/FlowAWR}{GraftOOD}.

</details>


### [88] [Stride: Scale-Aware Multi-Agent Evolutionary Refinement for Infographic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13502)
*Yan Zhou,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stride 是一种用于信息图生成的多代理迭代优化框架，借助独立的多模态“约束评估+视觉定位”验证器，按“全局-局部”尺度路由到作曲（全局改版）与修图（局部修补）子代理，多候选冗余与单调接受策略保证每步只在验证分提升时采纳，从而在多轮迭代中稳健提升约束满足度，并在多项基准上达到或超越专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息图生成需同时满足众多视觉、文本与语义约束；现有“生成-校验-细修”的代理式方法存在尺度错配：局部编辑难以修正整体排版/构图错误，整图重生又会破坏已正确区域；同时生成器具随机性、验证器不完美，单步决策不可靠，易回退。

Method: 提出 Stride：在冻结生成器上做“历史感知的进化式”迭代。将验证分解为两部分：1) 约束评估（是否满足）；2) 视觉落点（哪里/如何违背）。基于违背尺度进行路由：全球性问题交由“作曲”子代理做全局改版；局部问题交由“修图”子代理做局部修补。每个子代理内部先规划再生成多候选；采用单调接受（仅当验证分提升才采纳），以减少回退。通过尺度感知路由+每步冗余+单调接受，稳定积累改进。

Result: 在完全开源的生成-验证系统设定下，Stride 在 BizGenEval 超越包含 Nano-Banana-2.0、Seedream-5.0-Pro 在内的专有系统；在 IGenBench 上与 GPT-Image-2.0 持平并超过 Seedream-5.0-Pro；在 WISE 上达成新的 SOTA；并在所有评测中显著优于现有代理式方法。

Conclusion: 将约束评估与定位解耦，并按问题尺度路由到全局/局部子代理，辅以多候选冗余与单调接受，可在不更改底层生成器与验证器的前提下，实现信息图生成的稳健迭代优化，取得跨基准的领先表现。

Abstract: Infographic generation challenges text-to-image models because a single image must satisfy numerous visual, textual, and semantic constraints. Iterative agentic refinement with a multimodal verifier is a natural remedy, but existing methods face a scale mismatch: local edits cannot correct global composition errors, while full regeneration may destroy already-correct regions. We introduce Stride, a constraint-grounded multi-agent framework that treats refinement over any frozen generator as a history-aware evolution strategy. Stride separates constraint evaluation - whether each requirement is satisfied - from visual grounding - where and how it is violated. It then routes global revisions to a composer subagent and local corrections to a retoucher subagent. Each subagent conducts internal planning and generates multiple candidates, while Stride accepts a candidate only when it improves the verifier score, reducing regression across iterations. Because stochastic generators and imperfect verifiers make any single step unreliable, it is this combination of scale-aware routing, per-step redundancy, and monotonic acceptance that yields stable improvement across iterations. With fully open-weight generator-verifier systems, Stride outperforms proprietary systems including Nano-Banana-2.0 and Seedream-5.0-Pro on BizGenEval, matches GPT-Image-2.0 and surpasses Seedream-5.0-Pro on IGenBench, and achieves a new state-of-the-art score on WISE, while substantially outperforming existing agentic methods across all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [89] [SplatQ: Interaction-Aware Pruning of 3D Gaussian Splatting via Quadratic Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13503)
*Hongbin Ye,Haixu Liu,Shenwei Kang,Wenxiu Wei,Tinglan Wang,Hongdong Zhu,Fan Tang,Qi Gao,Yin Ma,Kai Wen,Hai Wei,Nuo Qian,Wei-Shi Zheng,Shaobo Chen,Fa-Ting Hong,Jingke Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatQ是一种为3D高斯贴片(3DGS)在固定预算下进行“区域感知+联合选择”的剪枝算法：先将高斯按空间与外观分块并限定块大小，给各块分配平滑后的预算，再用二次近似建模删除的联合效应，转化为QUBO由SA或CIM求解，实现更均衡、更高质、更快的渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS剪枝多以全局Top-K的独立重要性分数为依据，缺乏区域预算控制且忽视高斯间的联合删除效应，导致场景内质量不均衡、细节丢失和总体效果受限。需要一种同时考虑空间分配与高斯相互影响的组合优化方法。

Method: 1) 分块：依据空间邻近与外观相似性，将高斯划分为大小受限的块，把全局选择分解为若干子问题；2) 预算平滑：根据重要性为各块分配保留配额，并进行平滑以避免预算过度集中；3) 联合效应建模：用代表性像素颜色对高斯不透明度变化的响应来构建二次近似，刻画单体影响、成对交互及配额惩罚；4) QUBO求解：将目标写成QUBO，由模拟退火(SA)或相干Ising机器(CIM)进行联合选择；在固定总预算下输出保留集合。

Result: 在5个基准上，SplatQ在综合重建质量、空间质量均衡性与渲染速度方面均优于SOTA剪枝方法；消融研究显示：预算平滑提升局部细节，交互建模在固定预算下提升选择质量。

Conclusion: 将区域预算分配与高斯联合选择统一到QUBO框架，能在固定预算内实现更均衡、更高质量的3DGS剪枝；平滑预算与交互建模分别提升局部细节与整体选择效果。

Abstract: Existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pruning methods primarily rely on global Top-K selection based on independent importance scores, lacking regional budget control and ignoring joint deletion effects. Consequently, they cannot ensure balanced reconstruction quality across a scene. We propose SplatQ, a combinatorial pruning algorithm that coordinates regional budget allocation and joint Gaussian selection under a fixed budget. SplatQ partitions Gaussians by spatial proximity and appearance similarity into size-limited blocks, decomposing global selection into subproblems of a specific size. It then smooths importance-based block retention quotas to prevent excessive budget concentration. Finally, it constructs a quadratic approximation of joint deletion effects using the responses of representative pixel colors to changes in Gaussian opacity. Individual responses, pairwise interactions, and quota penalties are incorporated into a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) objective, solved using simulated annealing (SA) or a coherent Ising machine (CIM) to jointly select Gaussians. Experiments on 5 benchmarks demonstrate that SplatQ outperforms state-of-the-art pruning methods in aggregate reconstruction quality, spatial quality balance, and rendering speed. Ablations confirm that budget smoothing improves local detail reconstruction, while interaction modeling improves Gaussian selection quality under a fixed budget.

</details>


### [90] [S-Space: Exploring Spatial Workspace in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13527)
*Zhiyuan Shi,Xiang Li,Xingzhuo Guo,Shangchen Miao,Weiliang Chen,Qiyu Dai,Jinshan Ren,Jiangran Lyu,Fangfu Liu,Mingsheng Long,Jialong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现并刻画了MLLM内部一个线性“空间工作区”S-Space：对象token在其中沿水平、垂直、距离三轴连续编码位置；该空间可被感知写入、推理变换、语言读出，且对非空间任务相对独立。S-Space解释了模型何以能做空间判断，也揭示了其在视角变换推理上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在空间推理上表现出色，但其内部机制不明。作者希望回答：模型是否存在可解释的空间表示？这些表示如何被感知、推理与语言模块使用？其失效点何在？

Method: 通过对对象token激活求导，寻找能最大化空间答案分数的方向，识别出编码水平、垂直、距离的线性子空间S-Space；用因果干预（编辑S-Space投影）检验其对空间与非空间输出的影响；进行可读性测试（在非空间问句下读出位置）；分析方向词读出以诊断参考系耦合；构造感知-计算分解，区分表征形成与视角变换能力。

Result: 发现稳定存在的S-Space，能直接读出对象位置；感知阶段将位置信息写入其中，内部推理可在其中执行视角变换；语言模块选择性读取它。干预S-Space可改变空间判断而对非空间输出影响较小。方向词读出显示方位词把图像相对与地理方位耦合。分解分析显示：模型能形成高质量空间表征，但在跨视角所需的空间变换上不稳健。

Conclusion: MLLM内部存在可解释的线性空间工作区S-Space，支撑空间表征、视角变换与语言读出；其弱点在于空间变换环节。S-Space可作为诊断工具，揭示参考系耦合等系统性误差，并指向改进方向（更强的视角变换训练/归纳偏置）。

Abstract: Multimodal language models (MLLMs) exhibit promising spatial reasoning capabilities, yet the internal mechanisms supporting them remain poorly understood. We uncover S-Space, a linear representation subspace in which object-token representations encode continuous coordinates along horizontal, vertical, and distance axes. Although we identify these axes by differentiating spatial-answer scores with respect to object-token activations, S-Space is intrinsic rather than induced by specific data or prompts, remaining readable under non-spatial questions. We show that S-Space functions as an internal spatial workspace of MLLMs. Perception encodes object locations into it, forming spatial representations that can be directly read out to support accurate spatial judgments. Internal reasoning transforms it for changing viewpoints. Verbal responses selectively access it, evidenced by causal interventions that alter spatial judgments while largely preserving non-spatial responses. Case studies further show that S-Space assigns locations to unseen entities and the observer, and connects physical directions with abstract linguistic meanings, suggesting both the capabilities and limitations of large-scale language training. Finally, we use S-Space as a diagnostic tool for spatial reasoning failures. Direction-word readouts reveal systematic coupling between cardinal and image-relative reference frames. More importantly, a perception-computation decomposition exposes a key bottleneck: MLLMs can form capable spatial representations, yet fall short in the spatial transformations required to reason reliably across viewpoint changes.

</details>


### [91] [Knowing Whether, Not Just Where: Anchor-Probing Distillation for Open-Set Remote Sensing Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13535)
*Zhihao Chang,Lei Zhai,Shuyuan Yang,Zhixi Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnchorProbe-Distill(APD)以开放集遥感视觉指代，能同时定位并判断图像是否对查询提供视觉证据；通过教师-学生蒸馏，将“视觉支持”学习为核心，使模型能拒识缺失目标且提升已存在目标的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭集指代模型在目标缺失时仍给出似真框，混淆了定位置信与真实视觉支持，无法在开放集场景下判断“目标是否存在/被支持”。需要一种能从图像-文本对中学习“是否有充分证据”的方法，以实现统一的定位与拒识。

Method: 提出APD教师-学生框架，将开放集指代重构为视觉支持学习。教师(APD-T)利用“原始查询”作为参考锚点，通过对查询进行三类扰动来进行参考感知推理：1) 语义保持查询(Semantic-Preserving)应保持原标注目标；2) 近类歧义查询(Near-Category Ambiguous)依据是否形成稳定的替代锚或回落到参考锚来判定；3) 远类反事实查询(Far-Category Counterfactual)提供拒识监督。将这种锚点一致性/不一致性的判断蒸馏到学生(APD-S)，使其在单图-单查询下联合完成定位与拒识。

Result: 实验表明，APD能有效拒识图中缺失的查询目标，同时在目标存在时提升定位质量；与既有闭集方法相比，表现更稳健。

Conclusion: 通过将开放集视觉指代表述为“视觉支持”并利用参考锚的扰动推理与蒸馏，APD实现了统一的定位与拒识，在遥感场景中提升了开放集鲁棒性与定位精度。

Abstract: Open-set visual grounding in remote sensing requires models to determine not only where a queried object is located, but also whether the image provides sufficient evidence for the query at all. Existing closed-set grounding models, however, tend to return plausible boxes even for absent objects, blurring localization confidence with genuine Visual Support. We introduce AnchorProbe-Distill (APD), a teacher-student framework that recasts open-set grounding as Visual Support learning. APD builds on the insight that supported queries should induce coherent spatial anchors, whereas unsupported or mismatched queries tend to produce unstable or misleading localization patterns. APD-T therefore uses the Original Query as a Reference Anchor to interpret query perturbations: Semantic-Preserving Queries retain the annotated target, Near-Category Ambiguous Queries are resolved according to whether they form a reliable alternative anchor or collapse onto the Reference Anchor, and Far-Category Counterfactual Queries provide rejection supervision. This reference-aware reasoning is distilled into APD-S, enabling unified localization and rejection from a single image--query pair. Experiments show that APD enables the resulting model to reject absent targets while also improving its localization of present targets.

</details>


### [92] [Efficient Confidence-Aware Densification for 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13541)
*Manuel Hofer,Thomas Köhler,Felix Windisch,Bernhard Kerbl,Markus Steinberger,Lukas Radl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种在固定预算MCMC框架下的基于误差与置信度的稠密化策略，替代3DGS-MCMC的基于不透明度采样，使新原语更聚焦高误差/低置信区域，显著提升低至中高预算的PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS，并在无需先验的情况下逼近或超越含先验方法（ConeGS/EDGS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-MCMC使用不透明度驱动的采样与重定位，在固定原语预算下倾向将新原语分配到已重建良好的前景，导致背景欠重建；低预算时有先验的方法（ConeGS/EDGS）更优，但需额外预训练或初始化成本。作者希望在保持先验自由与固定预算优点的同时，重新引入“误差驱动”的稠密化以改善整体重建质量与资源分配。

Method: 在固定预算与逐步增长的MCMC框架中，用图像误差（屏幕空间梯度大处）而非不透明度来驱动新原语的采样与位置选择；引入学习到的置信度，优先在低置信（解释不佳）区域放置原语；同时识别并重定位对渲染贡献最小的原语到这些高误差/低置信位置，从而实现资源的自适应再分配。该流程既可纯先验自由运行，也可利用ConeGS或EDGS的初始化。

Result: 在24个场景的平均结果中，方法在100k至2M原语的每个预算上均优于3DGS-MCMC的PSNR、SSIM与LPIPS；在100k预算下，无需先验/初始化即可追回ConeGS相对3DGS-MCMC在PSNR上1.05dB优势中的0.84dB；从500k起，在比较的所有固定预算方法（含ConeGS/EDGS）中取得最高PSNR与SSIM；以ConeGS或EDGS初始化时，100k可匹配ConeGS的PSNR/SSIM，500k在三指标上最佳。

Conclusion: 误差与置信度感知的稠密化在固定预算MCMC框架下有效纠正了不透明度采样导致的前景偏置，提升了资源分配效率与重建质量；在无先验条件下全面超越现行标准3DGS-MCMC，并在利用先验时进一步刷新多项指标，显示其作为通用、可与先验协同的稠密化策略的优势。

Abstract: 3DGS-MCMC has replaced the gradient-driven density control of 3D Gaussian Splatting with opacity-based sampling and relocation, and is now the de facto standard for prior-free densification under a fixed primitive budget. Because opacity stays high where the scene is already well reconstructed, this sampling sends most new primitives to the foreground and leaves the background under-reconstructed. At low budgets, prior-based methods such as ConeGS and EDGS outperform it, at the cost of a pre-trained radiance field or dense image matches and a separate initialization stage. We argue for a comeback of error-driven densification within the fixed-budget MCMC framework. Our confidence-aware densification keeps the fixed budget and gradual growth of 3DGS-MCMC, but places primitives by the image error instead of the opacity. New primitives are drawn mostly where screen-space gradients are large. We favor primitives with a low learned confidence, which marks regions the reconstruction still explains poorly, and relocate the primitives that contribute least to the rendered images to these places. Averaged over 24 scenes, our method outperforms 3DGS-MCMC in PSNR, SSIM and LPIPS at every budget from 100k to 2M primitives. At 100k, without a prior or an initialization stage, it recovers 0.84dB of the 1.05dB PSNR by which ConeGS leads 3DGS-MCMC, and from 500k on, it has the highest PSNR and SSIM of all fixed-budget methods we compare with, including ConeGS and EDGS. While inherently prior-free, our densification can also exploit priors: from the ConeGS or EDGS initialization, it matches ConeGS in PSNR and SSIM at 100k and is best in all three metrics at 500k.

</details>


### [93] [MOSAIC: Multi-Scale Object-Centric Slot Adaptation with Image-Guided Prototype Conditioning for Remote Sensing Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13547)
*Liuqian Wang,Hanyu Xie,Rongzhen Zhao,Qi Ming,Chunjie Ma,Wentao Chao,Yufei Gao,Chengming Liu,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC：面向结构敏感图像复原（以遥感超分为例）的对象中心条件表示，用轻量残差调制适配冻结的扩散Transformer，取得更好PSNR/LPIPS与结构细节保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有把预训练扩散Transformer用于复原时，多以稠密低分辨率特征作为条件，容易把显著对象与背景纠缠，且对跨尺度/样本的重复结构抽象不足，难以处理结构敏感任务（小目标、重复纹理、布局边界）。

Method: 提出MOSAIC对象中心表示学习：将退化观测组织为结构slot，分别捕获对象级与布局级证据；跨分辨率整合；从共享原型记忆检索可复用先验以补足单图像不完整信息；将紧凑结构表示通过轻量残差调制注入冻结扩散Transformer的选定层，保留其生成先验。

Result: 在遥感超分（×2/×4）上实例化，数据集UCMerced、AID、RSSCN7上PSNR稳定提升；×4时LPIPS也更优；可视化显示小目标、结构边界与重复空间模式的保留更好。

Conclusion: 对象中心、原型检索驱动的紧凑结构条件能更有效地适配预训练扩散Transformer于结构敏感复原任务，在不破坏先验的前提下提升定量与感知质量。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion Transformers provide powerful generative priors for image restoration, yet adapting them to structure-sensitive tasks remains challenging. Existing methods mainly rely on dense low-resolution features as conditions, which often entangle salient objects with dominant backgrounds and provide limited abstraction for recurring structures across scales and samples. We propose MOSAIC, an object-centric representation learning framework that learns compact structural conditions for generative model adaptation. MOSAIC organizes degraded observations into structural slots that capture complementary object-level and layout-level evidence, integrates them across resolutions, and retrieves recurring priors from a shared prototype memory to complement incomplete image-specific information. The resulting representations are injected into selected layers of a frozen diffusion Transformer through lightweight residual modulation, preserving its pretrained generative prior. We instantiate MOSAIC for remote sensing image super-resolution, where small objects, repetitive structures, and large-scale variation provide a challenging testbed. Experiments on UCMerced, AID, and RSSCN7 show consistent PSNR improvements across $\times 2$ and $\times 4$ settings and favorable LPIPS results at $\times 4$. Qualitative results further demonstrate improved preservation of small objects, structural boundaries, and repetitive spatial patterns.

</details>


### [94] [Auditing VLM Benchmarks from Existing Evaluation Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13548)
*Denis Degtiarev,Anna Gromova,Sergey Katkov,Artem Mikhailin,Safina Mukhamedshina,Albert Nasybullin,Karina Shaikhutdinova,Artem Tuzov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在VLM评测中重用每题的预测与判定产物，通过一层“审计层”检测基准本身的问题：在VLMEvalKit上加入6个离线探测器与一次去图像的文本重跑，输出题目级报告与汇总统计。对MMStar审计发现约20.2%题目图像带来的总体准确率增益为零，其中17.0%可由题目匹配的置换零模型解释，仍剩+3.2百分点过量（校正后p=0.001），主要集中于数学与科学而非感知类；另有134题所有模型一致选同一非金标选项，更多出现在感知类。替换判分器为精确匹配不改结论；在二分类是/否基准上探测器失准，给出适用范围。并开源审计层、预测产物与报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM排行榜将每题细粒度产物压缩为单一分数，丢失可用于诊断数据集与评测可靠性的关键信息。作者希望系统化重用这些产物，对基准进行题目级审计：量化图像是否真正贡献、发现系统性误判模式、评估统计稳健性与模型池敏感性，从而提升多模态评测的可信度。

Method: 在VLMEvalKit上叠加“审计层”：
- 6个对已保存输出的后处理探测器；
- 一次仅文本（去图像）重跑；
- 按题生成报告与汇总统计（包括置换检验得到的“过量”量化、比例区间估计、对模型池的敏感性分析）。
对MMStar（声称图像必不可少的基准）用3个模型进行审计；使用按题匹配的置换零分布区分由偶然一致带来的零增益与真实问题；比较不同类别（数学、科学、感知等）；替换LLM判分为精确匹配以检验评分稳健性；在二元是/否基准上评估探测器校准度与适用边界。

Result: 在MMStar上：
- 20.2%题目图像带来的总体准确率增益为零；
- 置换零模型可解释17.0%，仍有+3.2百分点“过量”（校正后p=0.001），主要集中于数学与科学技术，感知类无此现象，暗示知识可替代视觉证据；
- 第二探测器标记134题：所有模型（能解析答案者）一致选择同一非金标选项，集中在感知类；
- 用精确匹配替代LLM判分不改变任何结论；所有评分差异均源自一模型输出难以解析；
- 在二元是/否基准上探测器表现失准，明确了适用范围。

Conclusion: 题目级审计能从现成评测产物中暴露基准问题与模型-图像依赖的错配，单一排行榜分数掩盖了重要信号。对MMStar发现相当比例题目不依赖图像或存在系统性干扰项，且该现象具有类别差异。审计层提供统计显著性与稳健性分析，可作为多模态评测的标准配套；但在二元题型上需谨慎使用或再校准。

Abstract: Every evaluation run of a vision-language model (VLM) already produces per-question artifacts (predictions, judged correctness, metadata) that leaderboards reduce to a single score and then discard. We reuse those artifacts to audit the benchmark itself, and add an audit layer to VLMEvalKit: six detectors that run as post-processing over saved outputs, plus one additional text-only rerun with visual inputs removed, emitting a per-question report. The layer reports per-question findings together with aggregate audit statistics; for the headline quantities, it attaches permutation-null excess where applicable, interval estimates for proportions, and sensitivity to the model pool. Auditing MMStar, a benchmark curated specifically so that the image is indispensable, with three models, we find zero aggregate accuracy gain from the image on 20.2% of its questions. An item-matched permutation null accounts for 17.0%, leaving a +3.2 pp excess (corrected permutation p = 0.001). This excess is concentrated in the mathematics and science categories and is absent from coarse perception, a localization consistent with knowledge substituting for visual evidence; confirming that mechanism would require item-level review. A second detector flags 134 questions on which every model that answered in parseable form picked the same non-gold option, and these candidates peak in the perception categories, whereas the zero-gain excess is largest in mathematics and science & technology. Replacing the LLM judge with exact string matching changes no conclusion, and every scoring discrepancy between the two traces to one model whose free-form answers a parser cannot read. On a binary yes/no benchmark the detectors measurably fall out of calibration, a scope boundary that we measure and report. The audit layer, the prediction artifacts and the generated reports accompany this submission.

</details>


### [95] [ELMA: Benchmarking Anaphoric Compositions for Long-Term Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13549)
*Shun Kato,Dixin Yang,Kotaro Amaya,Yuiga Wada,Kazumi Fukuda,Takashi Shibuya,Yuki Mitsufuji,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出针对含指代关系的多段文本指令，进行30秒级别的长时人类动作生成；发布自动化构建的ELMA数据集并微调现有模型，在物理真实感与语义对齐上显著提升，建立强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成多聚焦短片段，难以处理跨段依赖与指代（如前段设定的姿态或道具需在后段保持）。现实应用（数字化身、连续交互）要求长时一致性与情节化叙事建模，因此需要能理解并维护长期上下文的训练数据与方法。

Method: 构建ELMA：通过全自动抓取与过滤流水线，从野外视频中提取高质量、连续约30秒动作片段，并提供密集的分段级标注以保留跨段依赖与指代信息。基于该数据，对现有最先进动作生成模型进行微调，使其融入长程上下文。开展系统实验评估上下文依赖指令下的表现。

Result: 实验显示现有模型在处理含指代的多段指令时存在根本缺陷。使用ELMA微调后，在全局物理真实感与语义对齐两方面都有显著提升，能更好维持跨段一致性。

Conclusion: ELMA为长时、指代敏感的人体动作生成提供了关键数据与评测基线。结合ELMA微调可显著缓解现有模型的长程依赖问题，推动长时动作合成走向更稳定、可控与语义一致的方向。

Abstract: Text-guided long-term human motion generation is essential for applications such as continuously operating digital avatars. Despite recent progress on short clips, existing models struggle with multi-segment instructions that involve anaphora, where constraints established in earlier segments must be preserved over time. In this paper, we introduce a novel task of long-term motion generation from such anaphoric compositions. To mitigate this, we present ELMA, an episodic long-term motion dataset with dense segment-level annotations that preserve cross-segment dependencies. Built via a fully automated collection and filtering pipeline, ELMA extracts high-quality, continuous 30-second clips from in-the-wild videos, capturing the episodic narratives necessary for long-range state modeling. Leveraging this dataset, we fine-tune state-of-the-art motion generation models to incorporate long-range context. Extensive experiments reveal fundamental shortcomings in current models when handling context-dependent instructions. We demonstrate that ELMA enables significant improvements in both global physical realism and semantic alignment, setting a robust new baseline for long-term motion synthesis.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Holistic Scene Layout against View Shifts in Cross-View Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13553)
*Chao Yang,Songlian Li,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于跨视角地理定位的新方法：通过空间上下文建模与频域高频引导提升在大视角差与几何畸变下的匹配鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖判别式特征或显式几何对齐，但忽视了人类匹配场景时会综合多个地标的空间关系；同时，外观变化导致的特征不稳需要更鲁棒的结构线索支撑。

Method: 1) 空间上下文建模（SCM）：沿四个正交方向遍历特征图，在每个空间位置聚合四向（上下左右）方向表征并建模交互，形成更丰富的场景布局表示。2) 高频信息引导（HFIG）：从频域提取稳定的结构高频（边缘、轮廓）信息，强调对外观变化不敏感的线索，引导网络学习更鲁棒的视觉表征。两者联合用于跨视角匹配。

Result: 在标准跨视角地理定位基准上达到有竞争力的性能；在未知朝向与受限视场等更具挑战性的设置下表现出更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结合方向性空间上下文与频域高频结构线索，可显著增强跨视角地理定位对视角变化与外观差异的适应性，兼顾精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization remains challenging due to drastic appearance variations and geometric distortions caused by viewpoint changes. Existing methods primarily rely on discriminative feature learning or explicit geometric alignment, yet often overlook an important observation: humans tend to match scenes by integrating the spatial context of multiple landmarks rather than relying solely on isolated local visual cues. Inspired by this, we propose a Spatial Context Modeling (SCM) module, which traverses feature maps along four orthogonal directions and models interactions among the resulting directional representations at each spatial position. By aggregating complementary directional observations, SCM constructs richer scene layout representations. Since reliable spatial context depends on robust visual features, we introduce a High-Frequency Information Guidance (HFIG) module that extracts stable structural cues from the frequency domain. By emphasizing edges and contours that are less sensitive to appearance variations, HFIG encourages the model to learn more robust representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance on standard cross-view geo-localization benchmarks while exhibiting strong robustness under more challenging scenarios with unknown orientations and limited fields of view (FoVs).

</details>


### [97] [What We Talk About When We Talk About Emotion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13558)
*Xingzu Zhan,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出ROI-ReFT，通过结合视觉内容参考与情感反应变化，引导冻结的情感图像编辑器生成既保持源内容又达成目标情感的图像；在选定400条编辑请求上达成91.75%情感准确率与83.25%情感+内容联合成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感图像编辑需要在不破坏关键信息的前提下改变场景传达的情感；同一场景可支持多种情绪解读，编辑应依赖源图像语境。受情绪成分过程模型启发（将情绪体验与事件评价关联），作者探索以神经表征（fMRI编码模型TRIBE v2）作为情感编辑的参照，但现有编辑器的隐状态在视觉皮层解释度高、在情感相关脑区解释度低，呈失衡，促使提出新方法弥补。

Method: 1) 使用TRIBE v2 fMRI编码模型分析400对源-目标图像、8类情绪，比较EmoEdit、EmoEditor、AffectDelta的隐状态与预测脑反应在视觉区/情感区的一致性；2) 提出ROI-ReFT：将源图像的视觉反应作为内容参考，将目标情感的预测“情感反应变化”作为编辑方向，设计两条交互分支在生成过程中共同引导一个冻结的编辑器；3) 以生成图像的预测脑反应同时监督情感变化并约束视觉漂移；4) 用DINO-I相似度≥0.80评估内容保持，与目标情感识别共同构成联合成功指标。

Result: 在事后选择的400条编辑请求子集上：目标情感准确率91.75%；同时满足目标情感与内容保持（DINO-I≥0.80）的联合成功率83.25%。

Conclusion: 将神经预测作为双重信号（内容参考+情感方向）可有效提升情感图像编辑的情感达成与内容保真；现有编辑器偏重视觉而忽略情感脑区表征，ROI-ReFT缓解该失衡。代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Emotional image editing aims to change the emotion a scene conveys while preserving its essential content. Because the same scene can support different emotional interpretations, achieving an emotional goal requires visual changes grounded in the source context. Motivated by the Component Process Model, which links emotional responses to event appraisal, we investigate neural representations as a reference for these editing decisions. Using TRIBE v2, an fMRI encoding model, we analyze 400 source-target pairs across eight emotion categories. Hidden states from EmoEdit, EmoEditor, and AffectDelta correspond more strongly to predicted target-image responses in visual regions than in affect-related regions. This imbalance motivates ROI-ReFT, which combines source visual responses as a content reference with a predicted affective response change as an editing direction. Two interacting branches use both conditions to guide a frozen editor throughout generation. Predicted responses of the generated image supervise affective change and constrain visual drift. On a post hoc selected subset of 400 editing requests, ROI-ReFT achieves 91.75% target-emotion accuracy and 83.25% joint success, with the same output both matching the target emotion and reaching source-output DINO-I of at least 0.80. Our code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [98] [Thyroid Nodule Segmentation in Ultrasound Images with Conformal Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13604)
*Francesco Teodori,Pierpaolo Brutti,Umberto Ferraro Petrillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将DeepLabV3+、通道注意力与事后分割保序（conformal）预测结合的甲状腺结节超声分割框架，既提升分割精度又输出像素级不确定性；在TN3K上SOTA，对比中以CBAM+Dice-BCE最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺结节常见且部分为恶性；超声是首选影像，精确分割是后续CAD恶性度与转移风险评估的前提。现有分割方法多缺乏像素级不确定性与统计覆盖保证，且对多尺度上下文与注意力设计尚可优化。

Method: 以DeepLabV3+为主体，ASPP捕获多尺度信息，主干采用EfficientNet-B5；在编码器通道维度加入三种可选注意力（SE、CBAM、GCA）；比较两类损失（Dice-BCE与Boundary）。在训练后引入split conformal prediction，对模型输出构建像素级不确定性与覆盖保证的置信掩膜/热图。将分割作为包含恶性分类与转移风险预测管线的第一阶段。

Result: 在TN3K固定划分上系统评测并与现有方法直接对比：仅使用EfficientNet-B5的DeepLabV3+已优于已报道结果；加入注意力与损失对比后，CBAM+Dice-BCE组合表现最佳，整体达到或超过SOTA；同时提供像素级不确定性图。

Conclusion: 所提框架在精度与可靠性上均有改进：DeepLabV3+（EfficientNet-B5）结合CBAM与Dice-BCE取得最佳分割表现，事后保序预测提供具有统计覆盖保证的不确定性，有助于后续恶性度与转移风险的稳健评估。

Abstract: Thyroid nodules affect a large proportion of the general population. Although most are benign, a non-negligible fraction is malignant. Ultrasound imaging is the primary diagnostic modality, and accurate nodule segmentation is a prerequisite for CAD systems to classify malignancy and assess metastatic risk.\\ In this work, we propose a thyroid nodule segmentation framework that integrates a DeepLabV3+, channel-attention modules and post-hoc conformal prediction, providing both accurate mask and pixel-level uncertainty estimates. We address thyroid ultrasound nodule segmentation as the first stage of a pipeline that also includes malignancy classification and metastatic risk prediction. We adopt DeepLabV3+, whose Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling (ASPP) captures multi-scale context, with an EfficientNet-B5 backbone for its accuracy-efficiency trade-off.
